- **Bolt UX Design**: 🎨 UX & Frontend Design Authority - generates a distinctive design system, then crafts production-grade HTML interfaces aligned to it (anti-AI-slop, brand-driven) ([LuisFernandezFrias86/Bolt-brown](https://github.com/LuisFernandezFrias86/Bolt-brown/blob/HEAD/plugins/bolt-frontend/agents/bolt-ux-design.agent.md) · 5 copies)
- **bolt-adr**: Bolt ADR agent — Decision Logger. Creates Architecture Decision Records in MADR format for significant technical decisions. Use whenever a non-trivial trade-off is taken (framework choice, database, pattern, async vs sync, etc.). ([ava-group-iberiademos/BOLTMonitor](https://github.com/ava-group-iberiademos/BOLTMonitor/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/bolt-adr.md))
- **bolt-alignment**: Bolt Alignment agent — Business-Technical Alignment Analyst. Cross-phase agent that validates the Alignment Triangle (Business Goals ↔ Requirements ↔ Implementation) using OKRs, KPIs and metrics. Use periodically to detect strategic drift. ([ava-group-iberiademos/BOLTMonitor](https://github.com/ava-group-iberiademos/BOLTMonitor/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/bolt-alignment.md))
- **bolt-analyze**: Bolt Analyze agent — Quality Analyst. Runs consistency analysis across spec, data model, contracts, code and tests, producing a drift/alignment report. Use in VALIDATE phase before merging or releasing. ([ava-group-iberiademos/BOLTMonitor](https://github.com/ava-group-iberiademos/BOLTMonitor/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/bolt-analyze.md))
- **bolt-architect**: Bolt Architect agent — Solution Architect. ([ava-group-iberiademos/BOLTMonitor](https://github.com/ava-group-iberiademos/BOLTMonitor/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/bolt-architect.md))
- **bolt-bug-fixer**: Bolt Bug Fixer agent — orquestador del ciclo completo de corrección de bugs (registro → investigación → test RED → fix GREEN → validación → cierre). Coordina bolt-bug-tracker, bolt-bug-troubleshooter, bolt-regression-test y bolt-implement en secuencia estricta. ([ava-group-iberiademos/BOLTMonitor](https://github.com/ava-group-iberiademos/BOLTMonitor/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/bolt-bug-tracker.md))

Documenta toda la evidencia y produce hipótesis priorizadas. - **bolt-docs**: Bolt Docs agent — Documentation Lead. - **bolt-executor**: Use this agent when there is a clear, well-scoped task to implement in code — a feature, fix, or refactor with defined acceptance criteria. Bolt prefers the smallest viable change, runs verification after each step, and escalates to @apex-architect after 3 failed attempts on the same issue. Examples: - user: "add a timeout parameter to fetchData() with default 5000ms" assistant: "I will use Bolt to implement this with the smallest viable diff." &lt;commentary&gt;Clear, scoped task. Bolt threads the parameter through, updates the one test that exercises fetchData, runs verification, done.&lt;/commentar… ([evolution-foundation/evo-nexus](https://github.com/evolution-foundation/evo-nexus/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/bolt-executor.md) · ★559)
- **bolt-feature**: Bolt Feature agent — creates comprehensive feature specifications (requirements.md, user stories, ACs marked @smoke) and auto-provisions the feature branch. Use at INCEPTION / DISCOVERY phase when transforming a business idea into a structured spec. ([ava-group-iberiademos/BOLTMonitor](https://github.com/ava-group-iberiademos/BOLTMonitor/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/bolt-feature.md))
- **bolt-framework**: AI-Driven Development Lifecycle Orchestrator. Use to guide a project through the full Bolt Framework lifecycle (Inception → Discovery → Construction → Transition → Production → Retirement), detect the current phase, and route to the right specialized subagent. Triggers: 'Bolt Framework', 'where are we in the lifecycle', 'next phase', 'route to bolt agent', '/bolt'. ([ava-group-iberiademos/BOLTMonitor](https://github.com/ava-group-iberiademos/BOLTMonitor/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/bolt-framework.md))

([ava-group-iberiademos/BOLTMonitor](https://github.com/ava-group-iberiademos/BOLTMonitor/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/bolt-gherkin.md))

- **bolt-legacy-analyst**: Discovery de brownfield — lee el código en legacy/ y produce un assess ligero, un mapa (call graph / data lineage) y la extracción de reglas de negocio en Given/When/Then. Also invoke when resuming after a break, switching repos, or when context has been cleared. Creates ARCHITECTURE.md if missing. ([NicoLafakis/ProvenCLU](https://github.com/NicoLafakis/ProvenCLU/blob/HEAD/agents/bootstrap.md))

Se invoca desde agente `bossdream-qa-planner` (modo `Paso 0 de qa-increment`). - **bound_judge**: 你是代码改动的复核员。一张「期望表」被程序拿去调用改完后的代码，下面这些条目没通过。你要逐条判断是「代码错」还是「期望写错」。 判定标准只有一个：任务原文。 - 「代码错」：实际返回/行为不符合任务原文的要求（期望是对的，代码没做到）； - 「期望写错」：期望与任务原文不符，或者从任务原文推不出这个期望（例如把参数类型写错、把任务没要求的行为当成要求、夹具/输入理解错了、算错了结果）。 要求： 1. 每条必须给出依据，引任务原文里的原话，并说明实际结果为什么符合/不符合它； 2. 拿不准就判「代码错」； 3. 可以参考改动函数当前代码来理解实际结果是怎么来的，但判断标准是任务原文，不是代码现在怎么写。 只输出一个 JSON 数组，不要别的文字，每项：{"item": 数字, "verdict": "代码错" 或 "期望写错", "basis": "引任务原文 + 一句理由"} ([phoenix-phi0/agent_mcp](https://github.com/phoenix-phi0/agent_mcp/blob/HEAD/tools/dslagent/agents/bound_judge.md))
- **bound_table_fill**: {{插入 plan_rules_{包}_table.md}} ([phoenix-phi0/agent_mcp](https://github.com/phoenix-phi0/agent_mcp/blob/HEAD/tools/dslagent/agents/bound_table_fill.md))
- **boundaries**: - ✅ **Always:** - Write to `creators/`, `cypress/`, `docs/`, `docusaurus/`, `pkg/`, `scripts/`, `shell/` and `storybook/`. - Make commits in a new branch (for a PR). - Run tests before commits. - Follow existing naming conventions (PascalCase for components, camelCase for functions). - After changing a vue, js or ts file make sure it's automatically formatted with eslint - ⚠️ **Ask first:** - Addi ([rancher/dashboard](https://github.com/rancher/dashboard/blob/HEAD/docs/agents.md/agents/boundaries.md) · ★634)

[ADR 0075](../adr/0075-each-area-groups-code-by-behavior-first.md) groups the code of each area by behavior first. ([Anselmoo/werkstoff](https://github.com/Anselmoo/werkstoff/blob/HEAD/plugins/zirkel/agents/branch-proposer.md) · ★1)
- **branch-protection**: Owned by `security`. Applying or changing protection on `main` is a pass gate: security prepares the command, the user runs it. Origin: organism-infra/161 (158 security finding: the repo is public and `main` was unprotected). ([DerekHertz/DimSumDev](https://github.com/DerekHertz/DimSumDev/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/branch-protection.md))
- **branch-protection**: Read this before you change `.github/workflows/` or the required checks. `.claude/CLAUDE.md` → Git Workflow step 7 points here. ([soniCaH/www.kcvvelewijt.be](https://github.com/soniCaH/www.kcvvelewijt.be/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/branch-protection.md))
- **branch-protection-audit**: Audits branch protection configuration. Use when checking if a repo enforces required reviews, status checks, and prevents direct pushes to main. ([aboavent/payments-demo](https://github.com/aboavent/payments-demo/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/branch-protection-audit.md))
- **branch-protection-writer**: Generates scripts/setup-branch-protection.sh, a gh-CLI script the project owner runs ONCE after CI lands to enforce required status checks on main. Pulls the canonical required-check list from workflow-writer's output (CI / lint, CI / typecheck, CI / design-system-compliance, CI / unit-tests, E2E / feature, E2E / regression-core, E2E / visual, E2E / coverage-check, plus CI / db-schema-drift if DB present). ([Modern-Refactor/bytheslice](https://github.com/Modern-Refactor/bytheslice/blob/HEAD/skills/final-quality-check/agents/branch-protection-writer.md) · 2 copies)
- **branch-readiness-check**: Read-only diagnostic that returns a binary verdict (READY / NOT READY) on whether the current branch is ready to push. Checks: working copy clean, rebase status, commits coherent, related GitHub issues updated, pr-prep evidence, code review run, branch pushed. Use before `gh pr create`, before `git push`, or any time the user asks "is this branch ready?". ([holomush/holomush](https://github.com/holomush/holomush/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/branch-readiness-check.md))
- **branch-rebaser**: Rebases a feature branch onto the development branch: resolves conflicts, loads the Linear ticket, optimizes the code, lints (oxfmt/oxlint), tests and reviews. Batch mode also commits and force-pushes with lease. ([lenneTech/claude-code](https://github.com/lenneTech/claude-code/blob/HEAD/plugins/lt-dev/agents/branch-rebaser.md))
- **branch-reporter**: 汇总所有 active branch/worktree 的状态与依赖关系、生成 worktree 报告时使用；绝不操作 git 写动作。 ([kanydao/ml-template](https://github.com/kanydao/ml-template/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/branch-reporter.md) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **branch-researcher**: Use this agent to search JewishGen databases and other genealogy sources (Geni.com, Yad Vashem, Holocaust.cz, GenTeam) for a specific person or family branch. It runs unified and detail searches, parses results, and writes findings to a branch file with source citations. Assign it a specific person to research and a target branch file to write to. &lt;example&gt;user: Search JewishGen for Abraham Goldberg born 1885 in Krakow&lt;/example&gt; &lt;example&gt;user: Look for Levy marriage records in Moravia&lt;/example&gt; ([holocaustio/agent-plugins](https://github.com/holocaustio/agent-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/genealogy-research/agents/branch-researcher.md))
- **branch-reviewer**: Reviews a local branch or working tree that has no PR, running pr-reviewer's detection core and writing findings to `findings.jsonl` with zero GitHub calls; read-only. Dispatch with `Task(subagent_type="branch-reviewer", prompt="--base &lt;ref&gt; [--head &lt;ref&gt;] [--out &lt;path&gt;]")`; use `pr-reviewer` once a PR exists. ([mthines/agent-skills](https://github.com/mthines/agent-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/branch-reviewer.md) · ★21)
- **branch-reviewer**: Reviews the whole branch diff once, at the end of a flow (after every AC is green, before E2E), for correctness and consistency in a fresh context. Findings route back to review-fix only. ([MiladNalbandi/keel-v2](https://github.com/MiladNalbandi/keel-v2/blob/HEAD/content/agents/branch-reviewer.md) · ★13)
- **branch-reviewer**: Code review specialist for branch comparisons and PR preparation. Use PROACTIVELY when: (1) Reviewing branch changes before merge, (2) Comparing current branch vs main/develop, (3) Preparing code for PR submission, (4) Analyzing diffs for security/performance issues. ([juanmhidalgo/claude-plugins](https://github.com/juanmhidalgo/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/code-review/agents/branch-reviewer.md) · ★8)
- **branch-reviewer**: Reviews a whole plan-sdd branch along exactly one assigned dimension, or audits every task against its acceptance criteria. Read-only. Dispatched by the plan-sdd skill in phase 3; not for general use. ([zcl0621/boss-sdd](https://github.com/zcl0621/boss-sdd/blob/HEAD/skills/omp/agents/branch-reviewer.md) · ★4 · 2 copies)
- **branch-reviewer**: You are a specialist code reviewer focused exclusively on **readability of the branch as a whole**: whether the commits are structured so that another software engineer can review them efficiently. You do not review the content of the code (naming, design, security, smells — other agents handle those). You review how the branch *reads*, commit by commit. ([dimagi/dimagi-claude-workflows](https://github.com/dimagi/dimagi-claude-workflows/blob/HEAD/plugins/code-review/agents/branch-reviewer.md) · ★3)
- **branch-reviewer**: Audits all changes on the current git branch since its base for bugs, security, architecture, performance and maintainability problems. Use when the user wants a full review of a local feature branch before opening a PR or pushing ("review my branch", "audit this branch", "check the branch before I push"), asks what they have changed on the branch compared to main ("what have I changed locally"), or has a branch that has grown large and wants every changed file checked, tests included. Use proactively when Claude has just finished a multi-commit implementation on a feature branch and the user… ([DoctorMozg/claude-pipelines](https://github.com/DoctorMozg/claude-pipelines/blob/HEAD/plugins/mz-dev-git/agents/branch-reviewer.md) · ★3)
- **branch-reviewer**: Reviews one branch before its pull request opens, from the brief `scripts/pr-review.mjs brief --local` writes, on three dimensions - correctness against the acceptance criteria of the issues its title cites, maintainability (a product rubric and a context-engineering rubric) and blast radius - and reports what the session must fix before it pushes. The `open-pr` skill runs it before every push, so the review has a context of its own and not the author's. It is the only review of correctness and maintainability; the pull-request reviewer in CI decides by the high-risk floor alone. It reads onl… ([jasonroberts-tw/asdlc-openspec](https://github.com/jasonroberts-tw/asdlc-openspec/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/branch-reviewer.md) · ★2)
- **branch-reviewer**: Reviews a whole branch before merge and proposes knowledge-base improvements from the batch's audits and reviews. Use for the final whole-branch review of every batch. ([jblossey/houserules](https://github.com/jblossey/houserules/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/branch-reviewer.md) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **branch-reviewer**: One whole-branch review before ship, on five axes against the review checklist. Labelled findings; Critical or Required ones block and name their fix. ([jason-flenniken/cairn](https://github.com/jason-flenniken/cairn/blob/HEAD/plugins/cairn/agents/branch-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **branch-reviewer**: Read-only reviewer for /ship's local review and the review loop .harness/agent-rules.md states. Reads a branch diff the parent wrote under artifacts/review/, and the files and owners it touches, and returns findings as JSON. Has no capability to edit files, run shell commands, request the network, or spawn further agents — the diff is untrusted input, so the profile, not a prompt, is what keeps a prompt-injected line from acting. ([alexander-shamray/blueprint-admin](https://github.com/alexander-shamray/blueprint-admin/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/branch-reviewer.md) · 5 copies)
- **branch-reviewer**: Reviews a branch (or a single file) against this project's house rules and architectural patterns. Invoked by /branch-review; returns a structured verdict. ([antonis-zisis/my-wallet](https://github.com/antonis-zisis/my-wallet/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/branch-reviewer.md))
- **branch-reviewer**: SDD Stage 2 branch review — dispatch ONCE per branch, after all tasks pass task review and BEFORE creating the PR. Whole-branch merge review using the superpowers code-reviewer template. Requires BASE_SHA and HEAD_SHA. ([mizoo-snow21/dotfiles](https://github.com/mizoo-snow21/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/branch-reviewer.md))
- **branch-reviewer**: Read-only pre-merge review of a branch (or a whole feature) against main, with an auth-security and test-coverage focus. Use only when the user asks for a branch or feature review. ([Ori-Sason/cat-store-2026-semi](https://github.com/Ori-Sason/cat-store-2026-semi/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/branch-reviewer.md))
- **branch-scanner**: Prunes stale local git branches in a single repository — classifies SAFE vs NEEDS REVIEW, reads the diffs behind anything uncertain, and deletes the safe ones only when the prompt authorises it. Use when scanning or cleaning several repositories at once. Spawn one agent per repository. ([tehw0lf/claude-skills](https://github.com/tehw0lf/claude-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/prune-branches/agents/branch-scanner.md))
- **branch-setup**: PRFlow implement's Phase 1.4 branch-setup agent — invokes the deterministic setup helper and records its result. ([The01Geek/prflow](https://github.com/The01Geek/prflow/blob/HEAD/agents/branch-setup.md) · ★101)
- **branch-setup**: Première phase du workflow. Crée TOUJOURS une branche dédiée impl/&lt;slug&gt;-&lt;NN&gt; à partir de la branche par défaut du repo (ou de la base fournie) mise À JOUR via git fetch, avant tout autre travail. Deux modes : séquentiel (git switch -c dans le checkout de session, arbre propre exigé — STOP si sale) ou worktree (git worktree add dans un répertoire dédié hors de l'arbre suivi, arbre principal libre, pour l'exécution parallèle isolée). Ne crée aucun commit, n'écrit aucun fichier de code. Retourne la branche créée, sa base et, en mode worktree, le chemin absolu du worktree. Léger. ([sebc-dev/marketplace](https://github.com/sebc-dev/marketplace/blob/HEAD/scd-sdd/.claude/agents/branch-setup.md))
- **branch-setup**: Première phase du run. Crée TOUJOURS une branche dédiée `impl/&lt;slug&gt;-NN` à partir de la branche par défaut du dépôt (ou de la base fournie) mise À JOUR via `git fetch`, avant tout autre travail. Deux modes : séquentiel (`git switch -c` dans le checkout de session, arbre propre exigé — STOP si sale) ou worktree (`git worktree add` dans un répertoire dédié hors de l'arbre suivi, arbre principal libre, pour l'exécution parallèle isolée). Ne crée aucun commit, n'écrit aucun fichier de code. Retourne la branche créée, sa base et, en mode worktree, le chemin absolu du worktree. Léger. ([sebc-dev/marketplace](https://github.com/sebc-dev/marketplace/blob/HEAD/scd-spec-dev/.claude/agents/branch-setup.md))
- **branch-setup-worker**: Create git worktrees or branches for workspace isolation. Spawned by the iterative:implementing skill during setup phase. ([tmchow/tmc-marketplace](https://github.com/tmchow/tmc-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/iterative-engineering/agents/branch-setup-worker.md) · ★22)
- **branch-shepherd**: Autonomously delivers one or more review-clean branches — push, PR, CI, CodeRabbit, conflict reconciliation, squash-merge, cleanup. Dispatched with a branch list; reports one outcome table. ([jwp23/joe-bag-of-tricks](https://github.com/jwp23/joe-bag-of-tricks/blob/HEAD/plugins/joe-bag-of-tricks/agents/branch-shepherd.md))
- **branch-syncer**: Branch synchronization agent — merges the team base branch into the current feature branch before a PR, resolves merge conflicts with three-way evidence and intent analysis, verifies the merge with a build and targeted tests, and reports what was merged and how each conflict was resolved. Use when preparing a branch for a PR, when a PR has conflicts with its target, or when asked to sync/update a feature branch with developer/main. ([TheophilusChinomona/idev](https://github.com/TheophilusChinomona/idev/blob/HEAD/agents/branch-syncer.md))
- **branch-test-planner**: Especialista em cobertura de testes para mudanças do branch atual. Use para identificar testes ausentes antes do merge. Diferença vs @test-planner: este é DIFF-SCOPED (só as mudanças do branch, gate pré-PR); o test-planner é o planejamento sistemático geral (estratégia de cobertura do projeto). Mesmo padrão de branch-code-reviewer vs code-reviewer. ([marciocar/onion-plugins](https://github.com/marciocar/onion-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/onion-engineering/agents/branch-test-planner.md) · ★1)
- **branch-ticket-detector**: Infers the ticket/issue a developer is working on from the current git branch and worktree, then fetches its details. Used by /research-codebase (and optionally /design-doc, /create-plan) as a fallback when the user runs the command without naming a ticket or question.
